target_link_libraries(GmatQtFrontend PUBLIC Qt6::Widgets Qt6::OpenGLWidgets GmatBase GmatUtil ${OPENSCENEGRAPH_LIBRARIES})
target_link_libraries(GmatQtFrontend PRIVATE Qt6::Network)
target_include_directories(GmatQtFrontend PUBLIC ${OPENSCENEGRAPH_INCLUDE_DIRS})
get_filename_component(qt_osg_library_dir "${OSG_LIBRARY_RELEASE}" DIRECTORY)
target_compile_definitions(GmatQtFrontend PRIVATE
   GMAT_QT_OSG_PLUGIN_DIR="${qt_osg_library_dir}/osgPlugins-${OPENSCENEGRAPH_VERSION}")
target_include_directories(GmatQtFrontend PUBLIC ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR})
if(WIN32)
   # Engine headers expose Windows headers; retain the C++ min/max functions.
   target_compile_definitions(GmatQtFrontend PUBLIC NOMINMAX)
endif()
add_executable(GmatQt main.cpp)
find_package(Python3 QUIET COMPONENTS Interpreter)
if(Python3_Interpreter_FOUND)
   # Explicit, like runtime deployment: compiling the GUI does not require Java.
   # The documentation Makefile uses the repository's bundled DocBook tools.
   add_custom_target(build-qt-help
      COMMAND ${Python3_EXECUTABLE} ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/BuildHelp.py
      COMMENT "Building and installing the offline GMAT user guide" USES_TERMINAL VERBATIM)
endif()
target_link_libraries(GmatQt PRIVATE GmatQtFrontend)
_SETOUTPUTDIRECTORY(GmatQt bin)
set_target_properties(GmatQt PROPERTIES DEBUG_POSTFIX ${CMAKE_DEBUG_POSTFIX})
if(WIN32 AND TARGET Qt6::windeployqt)
   # Keep deployment explicit: normal builds do not copy a Qt installation.
   # GMAT's non-Qt dependencies and the MSVC redistributable remain prerequisites.
   add_custom_target(deploy-qt
      COMMAND Qt6::windeployqt
         $<IF:$<CONFIG:Debug>,--debug,--release>
         --no-compiler-runtime --no-translations
         "$<TARGET_FILE:GmatQt>"
      DEPENDS GmatQt
      COMMENT "Deploying Qt runtime libraries and plugins beside GmatQt"
      USES_TERMINAL VERBATIM)
endif()
if(APPLE)
   set_target_properties(GmatQt PROPERTIES INSTALL_RPATH "@loader_path/../Frameworks")
   install(TARGETS GmatQt DESTINATION ${GMAT_MAC_APPBUNDLE_PATH}/MacOS)
else()
   set_target_properties(GmatQt PROPERTIES INSTALL_RPATH "$ORIGIN")
   install(TARGETS GmatQt DESTINATION bin)
endif()

         "$<TARGET_FILE_DIR:GmatQt>" "$<TARGET_FILE_DIR:Qt6::Core>"

   endfunction()

         # Preserve these positive hypotheses/assertions as explicit coverage gaps.

               ${CMAKE_COMMAND} -E env QT_QPA_PLATFORM=xcb LIBGL_ALWAYS_SOFTWARE=1

               "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/tests/NasaSolverLeafCases.json" ${solver_leaf_case})
            set_tests_properties(QtGui.NativeNasaSolverNestingRejection${solver_leaf_case}
               PROPERTIES LABELS "qt-gui;qt-acceptance-native-conformance" TIMEOUT 120)
         endif()

            LABELS "qt-gui;qt-propagation-precision" TIMEOUT 180 RESOURCE_LOCK gmat_qt_engine)
      endforeach()
   endif()

   # One opt-in observer shared by every C++ test. Normal CTest runs leave it
   # dormant. It is deliberately not linked into GmatQt or its frontend library.
   add_library(GmatQtAcceptanceSupport OBJECT tests/AcceptanceSupport.cpp)
   add_dependencies(check-qt GmatQtNasaSummaryA1Tests)
   add_dependencies(check-qt GmatBrouwerRotationCovarianceTests GmatNasaDirectOrbitConversionTests)
   add_dependencies(check-qt GmatQtNasaPlanetodeticConversionTests)
   target_link_libraries(GmatQtAcceptanceSupport PRIVATE Qt6::Widgets)
   get_property(qt_acceptance_targets DIRECTORY PROPERTY BUILDSYSTEM_TARGETS)
   set(qt_acceptance_executables)
   foreach(qt_acceptance_target IN LISTS qt_acceptance_targets)
      if(qt_acceptance_target MATCHES "^GmatQt.*Tests$")
         target_sources(${qt_acceptance_target} PRIVATE $<TARGET_OBJECTS:GmatQtAcceptanceSupport>)
         list(APPEND qt_acceptance_executables ${qt_acceptance_target})
      endif()
   endforeach()
   add_executable(GmatQtAcceptanceProbe tests/AcceptanceObserverProbe.cpp $<TARGET_OBJECTS:GmatQtAcceptanceSupport>)
   target_link_libraries(GmatQtAcceptanceProbe PRIVATE Qt6::Widgets)
   add_dependencies(check-qt GmatQtNasaCommandLifecycleTests)
   add_custom_target(build-qt-acceptance DEPENDS GmatQt GmatQtAcceptanceProbe ${qt_acceptance_executables})
   set(qt_acceptance_snapshot)
   set(qt_acceptance_watch_options --visible --max-screenshots 0)
   if(CMAKE_SYSTEM_NAME STREQUAL "Linux")
      set(qt_acceptance_snapshot --snapshot)
      list(APPEND qt_acceptance_watch_options --start-minimized)
   endif()
   add_custom_target(check-qt-acceptance
      COMMAND ${CMAKE_CTEST_COMMAND} --test-dir "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}" -C "$<CONFIG>"
         -L "^qt-(acceptance|tutorial)-support$" -LE "^qt-gui$" --output-on-failure
      COMMAND "${Python3_EXECUTABLE}" "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/tests/RunAcceptance.py"
         --build-dir "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}" --config "$<CONFIG>" --max-screenshots 0 ${qt_acceptance_snapshot}
      DEPENDS build-qt-acceptance USES_TERMINAL VERBATIM)
   add_custom_target(watch-qt-acceptance
      COMMAND ${CMAKE_CTEST_COMMAND} --test-dir "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}" -C "$<CONFIG>"
         -L "^qt-(acceptance|tutorial)-support$" -LE "^qt-gui$" --output-on-failure
      COMMAND "${Python3_EXECUTABLE}" "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/tests/RunAcceptance.py"

            "${qt_test_startup}" "$<TARGET_LINKER_FILE:MatlabInterface>"
            "$<TARGET_FILE:GmatQtNasaSolverMenuCatalogueTests>" "${matlab_catalogue_context}")
      set_tests_properties(QtGui.NasaMatlabMissionCatalogue${matlab_catalogue_context} PROPERTIES
         LABELS "qt-gui;qt-matlab" TIMEOUT 240 RESOURCE_LOCK gmat_qt_engine
         ENVIRONMENT "GMAT_QT_CAPTURE_CHILD_OUTPUT=1"
         WORKING_DIRECTORY "$<TARGET_FILE_DIR:GmatQt>")
   endforeach()
   foreach(matlab_io_case Scalar ScalarGlobal Array String QuotedString NumericProperty StringProperty Object NoInput NoOutput Literals InOut LiteralRecovery LiteralClose UndeclaredOutput WrongAliasType)
      add_test(NAME QtGui.NasaMatlabFunctionIo${matlab_io_case}
         COMMAND "${Python3_EXECUTABLE}" "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/tests/RunNativeTest.py" "${QT_XVFB_RUN}"
            "${CMAKE_COMMAND}" -E env "QT_QPA_PLATFORM=xcb" "LIBGL_ALWAYS_SOFTWARE=1"
            "$<TARGET_FILE:GmatQtNasaMatlabFunctionIoTests>" "${qt_test_startup}" "$<TARGET_LINKER_FILE:MatlabInterface>" "${matlab_io_case}")
      set_tests_properties(QtGui.NasaMatlabFunctionIo${matlab_io_case} PROPERTIES
         LABELS "qt-gui;qt-matlab" TIMEOUT 240 RESOURCE_LOCK gmat_qt_engine
         ENVIRONMENT "GMAT_QT_CAPTURE_CHILD_OUTPUT=1"
         WORKING_DIRECTORY "$<TARGET_FILE_DIR:GmatQt>")
   endforeach()
   foreach(matlab_destination_case RealParameter StringParameter ArrayElement CartesianParameter Quaternion ThrustAngle1N1 ThrustAngle1N3 ThrustAngle1N4 ThrustAngle2N1 ThrustAngle2N3 ThrustAngle2N4 QuaternionShort QuaternionMatrix QuaternionString QuaternionComplex QuaternionSparse QuaternionND ArrayWholeSmall ArrayWholeLarge AssignmentEntryRealParameter DynamicArrayOutput DynamicArrayInput DynamicArrayInOut DynamicArrayAlias NestedArrayIndices ParameterArrayIndices LiteralArrayInput OutputIndexZero OutputIndexFraction OutputIndexBounds InputIndexBounds InputIndexZero InputIndexFraction AssignmentEntryStringParameter AssignmentEntryQuaternion AssignmentEntryNestedArrayIndices)
      add_test(NAME QtGui.NasaMatlabAssignmentDestination${matlab_destination_case}
         COMMAND "${Python3_EXECUTABLE}" "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/tests/RunNativeTest.py" "${QT_XVFB_RUN}"
            "${CMAKE_COMMAND}" -E env "QT_QPA_PLATFORM=xcb" "LIBGL_ALWAYS_SOFTWARE=1"
            "$<TARGET_FILE:GmatQtNasaMatlabAssignmentDestinationTests>" "${qt_test_startup}" "$<TARGET_LINKER_FILE:MatlabInterface>"
            "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/tests/NasaMatlabAssignmentDestinationCases.json" ${matlab_destination_case})
      set_tests_properties(QtGui.NasaMatlabAssignmentDestination${matlab_destination_case} PROPERTIES
         LABELS "qt-gui;qt-matlab" TIMEOUT 240 RESOURCE_LOCK gmat_qt_engine
         ENVIRONMENT "GMAT_QT_CAPTURE_CHILD_OUTPUT=1"
         WORKING_DIRECTORY "$<TARGET_FILE_DIR:GmatQt>")
   endforeach()
   foreach(matlab_destination_target check-qt build-qt-acceptance)
      if(TARGET ${matlab_destination_target})
         add_dependencies(${matlab_destination_target} GmatQtNasaMatlabAssignmentDestinationTests)
      endif()
   endforeach()
   foreach(named_assignment_family CallGmatFunction CallBuiltinGmatFunction)
      add_qt_test(QtGui.NasaAssignmentNamed${named_assignment_family} GmatQtNasaScriptEventCommandTests
         "${qt_test_startup}" "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/tests/NasaAssignmentNamed${named_assignment_family}Case.json" ${named_assignment_family})
   endforeach()
   add_executable(GmatFunctionCallLabelTests tests/FunctionCallLabelTests.cpp)
   target_link_libraries(GmatFunctionCallLabelTests PRIVATE GmatBase GmatUtil)
   add_test(NAME QtAcceptance.FunctionCallLabels COMMAND GmatFunctionCallLabelTests)
   set_tests_properties(QtAcceptance.FunctionCallLabels PROPERTIES LABELS "qt-support" TIMEOUT 30)
   foreach(label_support_target check-qt build-qt-acceptance)
      if(TARGET ${label_support_target})
         add_dependencies(${label_support_target} GmatFunctionCallLabelTests)
      endif()
   endforeach()
   add_test(NAME QtGui.NasaMatlabFunctionCreation
      COMMAND "${Python3_EXECUTABLE}" "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/tests/RunNativeTest.py" "${QT_XVFB_RUN}"
         "${CMAKE_COMMAND}" -E env "QT_QPA_PLATFORM=xcb" "LIBGL_ALWAYS_SOFTWARE=1"
         "$<TARGET_FILE:GmatQtNasaMatlabFunctionCreationTests>" "${qt_test_startup}" "$<TARGET_LINKER_FILE:MatlabInterface>")
   set_tests_properties(QtGui.NasaMatlabFunctionCreation PROPERTIES
      LABELS "qt-gui;qt-matlab" TIMEOUT 240 RESOURCE_LOCK gmat_qt_engine
      ENVIRONMENT "GMAT_QT_CAPTURE_CHILD_OUTPUT=1"
      WORKING_DIRECTORY "$<TARGET_FILE_DIR:GmatQt>")
   foreach(acceptance_target check-qt build-qt-acceptance)
      if(TARGET ${acceptance_target})
         add_dependencies(${acceptance_target} GmatQtNasaMatlabFunctionCreationTests)
         add_dependencies(${acceptance_target} GmatQtNasaMatlabFunctionIoTests GmatQtNasaMatlabInterfaceTreeTests)
         add_dependencies(${acceptance_target} GmatQtNasaMatlabMissionCallTests)
         add_dependencies(${acceptance_target} GmatQtNasaMatlabScriptEventCallTests)
         add_dependencies(${acceptance_target} GmatQtNasaFminconCallbackStartupTests)
         add_dependencies(${acceptance_target} GmatQtNasaFminconProtocolTests GmatQtNasaFminconIterationProtocolTests)
         add_dependencies(${acceptance_target} GmatQtNasaMatlabLibraryWriterTests)
      endif()
   endforeach()
endif()
